 Definitions
 釋義



 “AUD”                          Australian dollar, the legal currency of Australia
「澳元」                            澳大利亞元，澳大利亞法定貨幣

 “Audit Committee”              the audit committee of the Board
「審核委員會」                         董事會審核委員會

 “Board”                        the board of Directors of our Company
「董事會」                           本公司董事會

 “China” or “PRC”               the People’s Republic of China, which, for the purpose of this report and for
                                geographic reference only, excludes Hong Kong, the Macao Special
                                Administrative Region of the PRC and Taiwan
「中國」                            中華人民共和國，僅就本報告及地區參考而言，不包括香港、中國澳門特別
                                行政區及台灣

 “Company” or “our Company”     Greentown Service Group Co. Ltd. ( 綠 城 服 務 集 團 有 限 公 司 ), an exempted
                                company incorporated in the Cayman Islands with limited liability on 24
                                November 2014
「公司」或「本公司」                      綠城服務集團有限公司，一家於 2014 年 11 月 24 日於開曼群島註冊成立的獲
                                豁免有限公司

 “Director(s)”                  the director(s) of our Company
「董事」                            本公司董事

 “GFA”                          gross floor area
「建築面積」                          建築面積

 “Group”, “we”, “us” or “our”   our Company and its subsidiary(ies) from time to time or where the context so
                                requires, in respect of the period before our Company became the holding
                                company of our present subsidiaries, the business operated by such
                                subsidiaries or their predecessors (as the case may be)
「集團」、「本集團」或「我們」                 本公司及其不時的附屬公司，而倘文義所需，就本公司成為現有附屬公司的
                                控股公司前的期間，指該等附屬公司或其前身公司（視乎情況而定）經營的業
                                務

 “HK$”                          the lawful currency of Hong Kong
「港元」                            香港法定貨幣

 “Hong Kong” or “HK”            the Hong Kong Special Administrative Region of the PRC
「香港」                            中國香港特別行政區

Company Overview
公司概覽



The Group is a leading happy living service provider nationwide, and is    本集團是中國具領先地位的幸福生活服務商，
based on people’s full range of life scenes and the entire life cycle of   圍繞人的全生活場景及房地產的全生命週期，
real estate, and provides a wide range of services that are strongly       提 供 多 元 化、與 生 活 呈 現 強 關 聯 的 服 務，包
associated with life, which include four types of services, namely         括 物 業 服 務、園 區 服 務、諮 詢 服 務 及 科 技 服
property services, community living services, consulting services and      務四類業務，為住戶提供種類廣泛的產品及
technology services, and provide our residents with access to a broad      服 務 組 合，滿 足 客 戶、業 主 及 住 戶 不 斷 變 化
portfolio of products and services that address the evolving daily needs   的日常生活需求。
of our customers, property owners and residents.

OUR PRODUCT AND SERVICE PORTFOLIO                                          我們的產品及服務組合
•    Property services. We provide a range of high quality property        •   物業服務。我們提供一系列高品質的物
     management services, such as security, cleaning, gardening, and           業管理服務，例如我們統稱為「標準」物
     property repair and maintenance services, which we refer to               業管理服務的保安、保潔、園藝、物業
     collectively as “standard” property management services, and              維修及保養服務，並就該等服務向在管
     charge the residents and property owners of our managed                   物業的住戶及業主或房地產開發商收取
     properties or real estate developers management fees for such             服 務 費。於 2024 年 6 月 30 日，我 們 的 總
     services. As at 30 June 2024, we managed properties located in            在 管 合 同 建 築 面 積 達 481.7 百 萬 平 方 米
     203 cities across 31 provinces, direct-controlled municipalities          的物業，涵蓋居民高端住宅、政府基礎
     and autonomous regions across China with a total contracted               建設、商業寫字樓、城市綜合體等，覆
     GFA under management of 481.7 million sq.m. which cover                   蓋 全 中 國 31 個 省、直 轄 市 及 自 治 區 的
     properties such as high-end residential buildings, government             203 個城市。
     infrastructure, commercial office buildings and urban complexes.

•    Consulting services. We offer consulting services to real estate      •   諮詢服務。我們向房地產開發商及地方
     developers and local property management companies to                     物業管理公司提供諮詢服務，利用我們
     address various needs that arise during each major stage of their         的品牌及專業優勢滿足其在房地產開發
     real estate development cycles, capitalizing on our brand name            週 期 中 各 個 主 要 階 段 的 不 同 需 求。例
     and professional expertise. For example, we provide real estate           如，我們向房地產開發商提供在設計和
     developers with project planning, design management,                      開發階段的項目規劃、設計管理、建築
     construction management and marketing management                          管理及營銷管理諮詢服務，我們亦提供
     consulting services for projects in the design and development            示範單位管理服務，促進他們已開發項
     stages, and show flat management services to facilitate sales of          目的銷售。我們同時也就已交付物業，
     their developed projects. At the same time, we also provide               向房地產開發商及物業管理公司提供管
     management consulting services with respect to delivered                  理諮詢服務。
     properties to real estate developers and property management
     companies.

Management Discussion and Analysis
管理層討論和分析



The Group is a leading happy living service provider nationwide. The            本集團是全國居領先地位的幸福生活服務商，
main business scope covers property services, community living                  主 要 業 務 範 圍 涵 蓋 物 業 服 務、園 區 服 務、諮
services, consulting services and technology services. The Group                詢服務及科技服務。本集團始終堅持「服務讓
always adheres to the concept of “Service Makes Life Better”, and the           生活更美好」的理念，並以成為中國最具價值
strategic goal of the Group is to become the most valuable and happy            幸福生活服務商為戰略目標，堅持客戶為本
living service provider in China. The Group adheres to the customer-            品質為核的服務策略，堅持人本科技協同共
oriented and quality-oriented service strategy, the development                 進的發展策略，堅持精益運營效益改善的經
strategy on human-oriented technological synergy, the business                  營 策 略，堅 守 服 務 初 心，堅 持 服 務 創 新，在
strategy of improving the lean operation efficiency, and the original           發展中與員工同心、與業主同行、與社會同進。
intention of service and service innovation. The Group will complete
the same goal with employees, grow together with the property
owners, and make progress with the society.

     The Group conducts substantially all of its business in China, with most   本集團主要集中於中國經營業務，絕大部分
     of the transactions conducted in Renminbi. Therefore, the Group is         業務以人民幣進行，因而承受的外匯風險有

     Group has not employed any financial instruments for hedging               沖用途，也未訂立任何遠期外匯買賣合同以
     purposes or engaged in any forward foreign exchange contracts for          作外匯風險對沖用途。由於本公司附屬公司
     foreign exchange risk hedging purposes. As the businesses of MAG, a        MAG 的業務主要以澳元進行，因而澳元的貶
     subsidiary of the Company, are conducted primarily in Australian           值或升值及利率調整將會影響本集團的業績。
     dollars, the depreciation or appreciation of the Australian dollar and     本集團將堅持密切監察所涉及的匯率風險及
     the adjustment of interest rates will have impact on the Group’s           利率風險，積極與各大銀行探討外匯對沖方
     performance. The Group will continue to closely monitor its exposure       案，於有需要時利用衍生金融工具對沖所涉
     to exchange rate and interest rate risks and actively explore foreign      及的風險。
     exchange hedging options with major banks, and may employ

     1   BASIS OF PREPARATION                                                 1   編製基準
         Greentown Service Group Co. Ltd. (“the Company”) was                      根據開曼群島公司法（經修訂），綠城服
         incorporated in the Cayman Islands on 24 November 2014 as an              務 集 團 有 限 公 司（「本 公 司」）於 2014 年
         exempted company with limited liability under the Companies               11 月 24 日 於 開 曼 群 島 註 冊 成 立 為 獲 豁
         Act (as revised) of the Cayman Islands. The Company’s shares              免有限責任公司。本公司的股份於 2016
         were listed on the Main Board on the Stock Exchange of Hong               年 7 月 12 日在香港聯合交易所有限公司
         Kong Limited (the “Stock Exchange”) on 12 July 2016 (the                 （「聯交所」）主板上市（「上市」）。
         “Listing”).

         The interim financial report of the Company as at and for the six         本 公 司 於 2024 年 6 月 30 日 及 截 至 2024
         months ended 30 June 2024 comprises the Company and its                   年 6 月 30 日止六個月的中期財務報告涵
         subsidiaries (together referred to as the “Group”). The interim           蓋 本 公 司 及 其 附 屬 公 司（統 稱 為「本 集
         financial report has been prepared in accordance with the                 團」）。中期財務報告乃根據香港聯合交
         applicable disclosure provisions of the Rules Governing the               易所有限公司證券上市規則的適用披露
         Listing of Securities on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong                  條 文 編 製，包 括 符 合 香 港 會 計 師 公 會
         Limited, including compliance with Hong Kong Accounting                  （「香港會計師公會」）頒佈的香港會計準
         Standard (“HKAS”) 34, Interim Financial Reporting , issued by the         則（「香 港 會 計 準 則」）第 34 號 中 期 財 務
         Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public Accountants (“HKICPA”).           報告 。其於 2024 年 8 月 23 日獲授權刊發。
         It was authorised for issue on 23 August 2024.

         The interim financial report has been prepared in accordance             中期財務報告乃根據 2023 年年度財務報
         with the same accounting policies adopted in the 2023 annual             表採納相同會計政策編製，惟預期將於
         financial statements, except for the accounting policy changes           2024 年年度財務報表反映的會計政策變
         that are expected to be reflected in the 2024 annual financial           動則除外。任何會計政策的變動詳情載
         statements. Details of any changes in accounting policies are set        於附註 2。
         out in note 2.

         The preparation of an interim financial report in conformity with        編製符合香港會計準則第 34 號規定之中
         HKAS 34 requires management to make judgments, estimates                 期財務報告要求管理層作出判斷、估計
         and assumptions that affect the application of policies and              和假設，而該等判斷、估計和假設會影
         reported amounts of assets and liabilities, income and expenses          響政策的應用及按迄今年度的資產與負
         on a year to date basis. Actual results may differ from these            債、收入與開支的呈報金額。實際結果
         estimates.                                                               可能有別於該等估計。

         This interim financial report contains condensed consolidated            本中期財務報告包含簡明綜合財務報表
